<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65745">#65745</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Zyphra ZAYA1</td>
<td>ZAYA1-8B, ZAYA1-74B-preview</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65302">#65302</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Baidu Unlimited-OCR-2</td>
<td>DASH sparse attention</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64016">#64016</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Liquid AI LFM3, LFM3-VL, LFM3-Audio</td>
<td></td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65826">#65826</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65827">#65827</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65828">#65828</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>NVIDIA Canary-1B-v2</td>
<td>ASR and X↔En translation</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64215">#64215</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Ming3-ASR</td>
<td></td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63585">#63585</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>NVIDIA Parakeet TDT</td>
<td>Through the vllm-parakeet-plugin</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65005">#65005</a></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h2>Highlights</h2>
<h3>Extensible KV cache, on by default</h3>
<ul>
<li>On CUDA and ROCm, the KV cache is sized from memory actually free <strong>after</strong> CUDA graph capture and warmup, not from an estimate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Result:</strong> a larger cache (e.g. MiniMax-M3 on H200: 433k → 672k tokens), so throughput is higher and TTFT lower at high concurrency.</li>
<li><strong>Trade-off:</strong> per-token decode latency can rise on some models (TPOT +6–18% on some H200 models at concurrency 128). Opt out with <code>--gpu-memory-utilization 0.9</code> or <code>--no-enable-extensible-kv-cache</code>.</li>
<li>Hardened with fixes for allocator fragmentation, startup OOMs and KV connectors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67296">#67296</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67485">#67485</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66731">#66731</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66523">#66523</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66197">#66197</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65137">#65137</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66605">#66605</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66674">#66674</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Runner V2 only</h3>
<ul>
<li>Model Runner V1 is removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373">#63373</a>).</li>
<li>Dual-batch overlap now runs on MRV2, including with speculative decoding and DCP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61464">#61464</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Speculative decoding</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Suffix decoding</strong> runs on MRV2 with a GPU speculator; Arctic Inference is no longer needed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60339">#60339</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Custom speculators:</strong> declarative configs plus a <code>register_speculative_method</code> plugin API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63205">#63205</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CUDA graphs</strong> for MRV2 draft-model speculators (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60989">#60989</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Adaptive verification</strong> works with XPress (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65175">#65175</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Draft KV cache matches the target's dtype</strong>, so an FP8 target gets an FP8 draft cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65198">#65198</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Performance</h3>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Improvement</th>
<th>Hardware</th>
<th>Gain</th>
<th>PR</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Step5 NVFP4 MoE on TRTLLM-Gen</td>
<td>4×GB200</td>
<td>up to <strong>1.95×</strong> decode</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65152">#65152</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>GLM-5.3-Flash DeepGEMM FP8 MegaMoE</td>
<td>NVIDIA</td>
<td><strong>1.6×</strong> output throughput vs DEP4</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60895">#60895</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Muse Spark Open specialized decode (opt-in)</td>
<td>GB300</td>
<td><strong>1.5×</strong> single-stream decode</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64077">#64077</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Token-sharded sparse-MLA prefill under TP (default)</td>
<td>GB200</td>
<td>GLM-5.3 TTFT <strong>1.24–1.40×</strong> faster at 32K–128K</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63956">#63956</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>KV-balanced MLA decode for Kimi-K3 (opt-in)</td>
<td>GB300</td>
<td><strong>1.34–1.59×</strong> decode attention</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63638">#63638</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Qwen3.5 on breakable CUDA graphs (no torch.compile)</td>
<td>NVIDIA</td>
<td><strong>3.0–8.8%</strong> lower decode ITL</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51012">#51012</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>FlyDSL decode monokernel, Muse Spark Open DSpark</td>
<td>8×MI355X</td>
<td><strong>+35–57%</strong> interactivity at conc. 1–8</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63440">#63440</a></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<h3>AMD ROCm</h3>
<ul>
<li>Release images and wheels are <strong>ROCm 10.0 only</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939">#61939</a>).</li>
<li>MoRI EPv2 via <code>--all2all-backend mori</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61361">#61361</a>).</li>
<li>Kimi-K3 specialized decode on MI355X, opt-in (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61435">#61435</a>).</li>
<li>TRITON_ATTN is the default attention backend on RDNA3 and RDNA4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60418">#60418</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Serving and operations</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Checkpoint/restore</strong> (CRIU + <code>cuda-checkpoint</code>) through <code>/checkpoint/prepare</code>, <code>/checkpoint/status</code> and <code>/checkpoint/restore</code>, in both the Python and Rust frontends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62570">#62570</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fault-tolerant DP+EP scale-down:</strong> dead ranks are removed and their experts redistributed without a restart (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46370">#46370</a>).</li>
<li>Best-effort <strong>NUMA binding</strong> on by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61258">#61258</a>).</li>
<li>Opt-in <strong>cache-aware admission</strong> ordering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54625">#54625</a>).</li>
<li>Rust frontend: stateless <strong>Responses API</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63902">#63902</a>) and <strong><code>n &gt; 1</code></strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65625">#65625</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Install</h2>
<p><strong>Python wheels</strong></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Platform</th>
<th>Install</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>CUDA 13.0 (PyPI)</td>
<td><code>pip install vllm</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CUDA 13.0 (uv)</td>
<td><code>uv pip install vllm --torch-backend=auto</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ROCm 10.0</td>
<td><code>pip install vllm --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/rocm/0.34.0/rocm100</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>XPU</td>
<td><code>uv pip install vllm --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/0.34.0/xpu --extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/xpu --index-strategy unsafe-best-match</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<p><strong>Docker images</strong></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Platform</th>
<th>Image</th>
<th>Short alias</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>CUDA 13.0 (default)</td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm-openai:v0.34.0</code></td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm:v0.34.0</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CUDA 13.0 + DeepEP over AWS EFA</td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm-openai:v0.34.0-efa</code></td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm:v0.34.0-efa</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ROCm 10.0</td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm-openai-rocm:v0.34.0</code></td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm:v0.34.0-rocm</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CPU</td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm-openai-cpu:v0.34.0</code></td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm:v0.34.0-cpu</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>XPU</td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm-openai-xpu:v0.34.0</code></td>
<td><code>vllm/vllm:v0.34.0-xpu</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p><code>latest</code> tags work the same way (e.g. <code>vllm/vllm:latest-rocm</code>). The default CUDA image now includes the AWS EFA userspace (libfabric, aws-ofi-nccl). The <code>-efa</code> image adds DeepEP built for EFA and routes NCCL over EFA by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65576">#65576</a>).</p>
<p>The <strong>Assets</strong> section below has the source tarball and the CUDA 13.0 (x86_64, arm64), CPU (x86_64, arm64, macOS) and XPU (x86_64) wheels.</p>
<h2>Full changelog</h2>
<details>
<b>Model support</b>
<ul>
<li><strong>Mistral Large 4</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64534">#64534</a>) runs on MRV2 with:
<ul>
<li>sparse MLA plus the DSA indexer, and cross-layer KV sharing;</li>
<li>DSpark, DCP/PCP, NIXL P/D and HiSparse.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Other new models:</strong>
<ul>
<li>MiniMax-M3.1-Flash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64111">#64111</a>), plus P/D across different prefill and decode TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64475">#64475</a>).</li>
<li>Nemotron-3.5-Transcribe with MTP and DFlash2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64282">#64282</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64944">#64944</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>DeepSeek-V4 / V4.1</strong>
<ul>
<li>Prefill gathers compressed KV once per KV-source group: 38 → 4 gathers per step (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59131">#59131</a>).</li>
<li>Decoder replay graphs share the model graph pool: prefill graph memory 15.52 → 9.88 GiB per rank on GB200 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63155">#63155</a>).</li>
<li><code>fused_inv_rope_fp8_quant</code> 3.5× faster, prefill 6% faster (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63540">#63540</a>).</li>
<li>DSpark/MTP memory savings of about 512 MiB per GPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63996">#63996</a>).</li>
<li>Engram host tables shared across DP ranks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62359">#62359</a>).</li>
<li>DeepGEMM sparse MQA logits on the FP8 indexer cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62011">#62011</a>).</li>
<li>Packed multi-image vision encoding, 1.6–2.6× (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65030">#65030</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA on the language model, compressor and <code>wo_a</code> layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57245">#57245</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64880">#64880</a>).</li>
<li>DSpark with PCP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61994">#61994</a>); PCP with PP or DP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63098">#63098</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: sparse prefill in FULL CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59986">#59986</a>), prefill memory budgeting (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63047">#63047</a>), MTP embedding under PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65816">#65816</a>), DeepSeek-V3.2 PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59961">#59961</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>GLM</strong>
<ul>
<li>GLM-5.3-Flash: DeepGEMM FP8 MegaMoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60895">#60895</a>), adaptive verification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60229">#60229</a>), row-strided QKV into FlashKDA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61614">#61614</a>).</li>
<li>GLM-5.3 FP8 MoE sorted-A TMA on H200, 6.0–7.6% E2E throughput (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64760">#64760</a>).</li>
<li>FP8 KV cache for DSpark and DFlash drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63841">#63841</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64612">#64612</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65553">#65553</a>).</li>
<li>FP32 indexer MQA logits restored for GLM-5/GLM-5.3/HY-V4, with new <code>--attention-config.indexer_logits_dtype</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64580">#64580</a>).</li>
<li>Fused KDA convs fix stale weights after RL refit (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60991">#60991</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Muse Spark</strong>
<ul>
<li>Opt-in Muse Spark Open specialized decode on GB300 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64077">#64077</a>).</li>
<li>One RoPE cache shared across layers: 11.5 GiB per GPU saved, 14.5% more KV cache on 8×B200 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65206">#65206</a>).</li>
<li>GEMM and communication+norm fusions (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65438">#65438</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64638">#64638</a>); hybrid DeepGEMM MegaMoE / TRT-LLM for NVFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64525">#64525</a>).</li>
<li>CUDA routing kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64553">#64553</a>), MegaMoE JIT warmup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63920">#63920</a>), DSpark draft loading fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65125">#65125</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>MiniMax-M3 / M3.1</strong>
<ul>
<li>Sequence parallelism and GEMM-RS for TP + MegaMoE, 14–20% lower P50 TTFT (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64659">#64659</a>), also with PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65537">#65537</a>).</li>
<li>Fused norm + MXFP8 quant, 1.39–1.60× (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64852">#64852</a>).</li>
<li>Triton query-tiled sparse prefill on Hopper (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57420">#57420</a>); SM90 split-K indexer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64037">#64037</a>).</li>
<li>No host sync in prefill metadata (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65883">#65883</a>); NVFP4 MegaMoE warmup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66227">#66227</a>); fmha_sm100 indexer updates (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66228">#66228</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: M3.1 prefill workspace (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64856">#64856</a>), sequence parallelism (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65496">#65496</a>), encoder CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65226">#65226</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Kimi</strong>
<ul>
<li>Opt-in <code>--attention-backend KIMI_K3_KV_BALANCED_MLA</code> for agentic workloads on Blackwell (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63638">#63638</a>).</li>
<li>Kimi-K3 MegaMoE symmetric buffer 10.73 → 2.50 GiB per GPU at TP8 EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63541">#63541</a>).</li>
<li>FlashInfer fused FP8 chunked-context K/V pack (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61527">#61527</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: KDA prefill OOM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63989">#63989</a>), NaNs via FlashKDA/FlashMLA pins (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64759">#64759</a>), sleep mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64583">#64583</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66273">#66273</a>).</li>
<li>Kimi K3.1 groundwork landed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66030">#66030</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66028">#66028</a>), but <strong>K3.1 is not servable in this release</strong>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Qwen</strong>
<ul>
<li>Qwen3.5: breakable CUDA graphs instead of torch.compile (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51012">#51012</a>); fused norm/gate + quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64509">#64509</a>) and AR + norm + quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64723">#64723</a>); GDN <code>in_proj</code> overlap (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64425">#64425</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64999">#64999</a>).</li>
<li>GDN prefill state gather/scatter fused for Qwen3-Next/Qwen3.5 and Kimi KDA, 1.16–1.50× (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66064">#66064</a>).</li>
<li>Qwen3.8-Flash-Next: PLE disk offload via <code>--engram-config '{"disk_offload": true}'</code> (27 GiB less on DGX Spark, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59958">#59958</a>), PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60234">#60234</a>), shared QSA top-k buffer (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65619">#65619</a>), loading fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62318">#62318</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65812">#65812</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64820">#64820</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60490">#60490</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: long-context int32 overflow (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63761">#63761</a>), multi-layer MTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62111">#62111</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Other models</strong>
<ul>
<li>Step5 MegaMoE with PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65504">#65504</a>) and KV block sizing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65153">#65153</a>).</li>
<li>Gemma 4: multimodal encoder DP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60314">#60314</a>), PP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65989">#65989</a>), FA4 FP8 MMA on SM90 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60372">#60372</a>).</li>
<li>Moved to the Transformers modeling backend: OLMoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63384">#63384</a>), Gemma v1 and StableLM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66393">#66393</a>), Aria (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59767">#59767</a>; temporarily about 11% less KV cache and 25% lower image throughput).</li>
<li>Matryoshka dimensions for EmbeddingGemma 2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64885">#64885</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60978">#60978</a>); RADIO C-RADIO v2/v3/v4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58468">#58468</a>).</li>
<li>Qwen3-ASR on the upstream Transformers config; no <code>--hf-overrides</code> needed for the ForceAligner (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66097">#66097</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63274">#63274</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: GPT-OSS MXFP4 LoRA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65825">#65825</a>), PLaMo3 GPTQ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62836">#62836</a>), Ling3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65088">#65088</a>), DomynEdge (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66819">#66819</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Multimodal</strong>
<ul>
<li>Encoder CUDA graphs for Pixtral/Mistral3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56277">#56277</a>), shared capture buffers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65084">#65084</a>), one replay per batch up to <code>encoder_cudagraph_max_token_budget</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65227">#65227</a>).</li>
<li>On-device input normalization for Nemotron VL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57924">#57924</a>), Gemma 3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64487">#64487</a>), Gemma 4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65577">#65577</a>).</li>
<li><code>--mm-encoder-tp-mode data</code> for MiMo-V2.6 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64179">#64179</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: FlashInfer ViT attention on GLM-4.1V/GLM-OCR/GLM-5.3-Flash (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65778">#65778</a>), Qwen3-Omni and Qwen2.5-Omni video budgets (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66017">#66017</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66190">#66190</a>), dots_ocr with TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64543">#64543</a>), embedding-only startup (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65689">#65689</a>), media cache eviction (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65363">#65363</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<b>Engine core</b>
<ul>
<li><strong>Extensible KV cache</strong>
<ul>
<li>On by default on CUDA and ROCm (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a>).</li>
<li>Headroom for measured allocator fragmentation, validated on Qwen3.5-397B-A17B-FP8 at TP8+EP on 8×H200 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67296">#67296</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: sizing and startup OOMs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67485">#67485</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66523">#66523</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66731">#66731</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66197">#66197</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65554">#65554</a>), connectors that failed to start (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65225">#65225</a>), up to 84% undersized caches on DGX Spark (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65413">#65413</a>), <code>--max-model-len auto</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65463">#65463</a>).</li>
<li>Startup warning when an explicit <code>gpu_memory_utilization</code> withholds memory from it (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66704">#66704</a>); docs on when to set it (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67071">#67071</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V2</strong>
<ul>
<li>The only runner after MRV1's removal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373">#63373</a>); DBO with spec decode and DCP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61464">#61464</a>).</li>
<li>Separate prefill (token) and decode (request) CUDA graph capture sizes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63406">#63406</a>).</li>
<li>Staged writes copied straight into UVA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63633">#63633</a>); idle sampler writes skipped, about 10–11% faster small-batch decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62748">#62748</a>).</li>
<li>Sampler warmed up after graph capture to stop memory growth under load (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62560">#62560</a>).</li>
<li>Lower peak memory for chunked prompt logprobs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64841">#64841</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64914">#64914</a>); Triton ReplaySSM (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65033">#65033</a>).</li>
<li>NVIDIA Confidential Computing decode fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59981">#59981</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Speculative decoding</strong>
<ul>
<li>Suffix decoding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60339">#60339</a>) and custom speculators (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63205">#63205</a>) on MRV2; draft-model CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60989">#60989</a>).</li>
<li>XPress with adaptive verification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65175">#65175</a>); adaptive verification draft budgets on a Triton kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63421">#63421</a>).</li>
<li>Batch-sharded draft logits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63554">#63554</a>); DSpark sequential blocks (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63886">#63886</a>).</li>
<li>DSpark sliding-window MLA draft layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63795">#63795</a>) under DCP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63796">#63796</a>); fused Markov sampling, 42–50% less sampling time (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64476">#64476</a>); <code>draft_lm_head_quantization</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64368">#64368</a>).</li>
<li>Draft KV cache dtype aligned with the target (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65198">#65198</a>).</li>
<li>Trace replay with ngram/EAGLE3/MTP, 1.47–2.03× (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63275">#63275</a>); 3× faster resample kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65100">#65100</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: DFlash acceptance of 0 with FULL CUDA graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64059">#64059</a>), a resample race (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66048">#66048</a>), acceptance-estimator cancellation (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65229">#65229</a>), standalone draft prefill under FULL graphs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65804">#65804</a>), grammar-invalid drafts (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61557">#61557</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Scheduler and engine overhead</strong>
<ul>
<li>Prompt token ids kept as int32 buffers: 40× less per-request CPU on a 128K prompt (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62702">#62702</a>).</li>
<li>Per-request id lists kept out of the cyclic GC (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65300">#65300</a>); 45–50% less KV block allocation CPU (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65831">#65831</a>).</li>
<li>New requests and aborts handled while waiting for model output (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65466">#65466</a>), with a cheaper wait loop (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65773">#65773</a>).</li>
<li>Opt-in cache-aware admission via <code>--cache-aware-admission-window</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54625">#54625</a>).</li>
<li><code>--max-num-inflight-kv-loads</code> starts async KV loads while the batch is full (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65319">#65319</a>), with cached reservations: 47.2 → 5.4 ms per schedule (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65285">#65285</a>).</li>
<li>A multimodal item is no longer split by a prefix hit (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65271">#65271</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Startup and compile</strong>
<ul>
<li>No openai/fastapi imports in EngineCore and workers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65423">#65423</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65589">#65589</a>).</li>
<li>CUDA graph memory profile skipped when its estimate is disabled (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64622">#64622</a>).</li>
<li>Device-neutral compile cache via <code>VLLM_COMPILE_ON_ONE_RANK</code>, on by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63247">#63247</a>); all graph-affecting config fields hashed into compile cache keys (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65652">#65652</a>).</li>
<li><code>--shuffle-safetensors-files</code> for large EP startups (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/42837">#42837</a>).</li>
<li><code>BasevLLMParameter</code>'s Python <code>__torch_function__</code> dropped: GLM-5.2-FP8 TTFT −6.1% on GB300 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63785">#63785</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Lifecycle</strong>
<ul>
<li>Process checkpoint/restore over HTTP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62570">#62570</a>); opt-in multi-worker CUDA checkpoint sleep (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64186">#64186</a>).</li>
<li>Best-effort NUMA binding by default; <code>--no-numa-bind</code> to disable (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61258">#61258</a>).</li>
<li>Sleep mode with exact-size pinned backups, up to about 578 GiB less host memory per node on Mistral Large 4 TP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64630">#64630</a>); level 2 discards only parameters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66010">#66010</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66165">#66165</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59994">#59994</a>).</li>
<li>Fast Start weight-cache restarts for online FP8/MXFP8, ModelOpt CUTLASS FP8/NVFP4/block-FP8 and AWQ/GPTQ Marlin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63672">#63672</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63754">#63754</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64119">#64119</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64182">#64182</a>), with pluggable shared warmup-cache stores (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65168">#65168</a>).</li>
<li><code>WatermarkConfig.enforce</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62966">#62966</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>RL and weight loading</strong>
<ul>
<li><code>VLLM_RELOAD_MODE=direct</code> writes checkpoint tensors straight into the live model (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64336">#64336</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64352">#64352</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65281">#65281</a>).</li>
<li>Every load and update finishes through <code>finalize_model_weights</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65914">#65914</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64357">#64357</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64692">#64692</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57678">#57678</a>).</li>
<li>Load plans for weight-transfer engines (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65235">#65235</a>); per-update NCCL source ranks for PP trainers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62557">#62557</a>).</li>
<li>Sparse weight patches validated in one pass, 40–48% shorter pause (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66021">#66021</a>).</li>
<li>Offloaded KV tier survives pause/sleep (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57810">#57810</a>); partial stacked weights rejected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63752">#63752</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>LoRA</strong>
<ul>
<li>MoE-LoRA shrink/expand kernels up to 64 tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64653">#64653</a>); embedding LoRA scaled by <code>embed_scale</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62470">#62470</a>).</li>
<li>Rejected: adapters on unwrapped modules (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63489">#63489</a>), non-linear expert placement with EP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62797">#62797</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: reloads with requests in flight (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64616">#64616</a>), KV-sharing fast prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61162">#61162</a>), SEQ_CLS heads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63286">#63286</a>), fused MoE expand rounding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63894">#63894</a>), Mamba-1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64681">#64681</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Batch invariance:</strong> LFM2 ShortConv (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62367">#62367</a>), FlashInfer FP8 bmm and W4A16 NVFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62192">#62192</a>), GPTQ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60479">#60479</a>), FA sliding window (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65627">#65627</a>), <code>aten::mm</code> out_dtype overloads (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60047">#60047</a>).</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<b>Large-scale serving</b>
<ul>
<li><strong>Expert parallelism and fault tolerance</strong>
<ul>
<li>DP+EP scale-down via <code>POST /v1/fault_tolerance/apply</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46370">#46370</a>); MoRI EPv2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61361">#61361</a>).</li>
<li>Opt-in <code>symm_mem</code> EPLB weight-migration backend (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62009">#62009</a>).</li>
<li><code>--enable-ep-weight-filter</code> with EPLB: DeepSeek-R1 NVFP4 DEP8 reads 105 GiB per rank instead of 385 GiB (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64187">#64187</a>).</li>
<li><code>num_redundant_experts</code> defaults to the minimum valid value (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30614">#30614</a>).</li>
<li>Skipped combine staging copy in <code>flashinfer_nvlink_one_sided</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65498">#65498</a>); DeepEP V2 padded rows (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54109">#54109</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Context parallelism and sparse attention</strong>
<ul>
<li>Token-sharded sparse-MLA prefill under TP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63956">#63956</a>); DCP on the FlashMLA sparse bf16 KV path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61609">#61609</a>).</li>
<li>MLA decode up-projected before the A2A merge with <code>--dcp-comm-backend a2a</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64994">#64994</a>); faster DCP sparse-indexer top-k (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65178">#65178</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64129">#64129</a>).</li>
<li>Cross-layer KV cache sharing for MLA layers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64095">#64095</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: PP rank derivation under PCP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65539">#65539</a>), FlashInfer MLA DCP decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59392">#59392</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>KV connectors</strong>
<ul>
<li>NIXL: push mode via <code>transfer_mode</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64230">#64230</a>); <code>ucx_backend_params</code> (rocm_ipc on MI355X: 3.4 → 483.7 Gbps, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62905">#62905</a>); 38% fewer hybrid SSM transfer bytes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62317">#62317</a>); block pairing across different P/D block sizes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59746">#59746</a>), with mismatched push handshakes rejected (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59747">#59747</a>); same-host NVLink with the extensible KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65137">#65137</a>).</li>
<li>Mooncake: selective leases (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63420">#63420</a>), automatic <code>cache_prefix</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65160">#65160</a>), per-GPU RDMA devices (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65076">#65076</a>), heterogeneous-TP hybrid sharing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54307">#54307</a>), sleep mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59934">#59934</a>), lease renewal (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64193">#64193</a>).</li>
<li>Lookahead block hashes in NIXL, Mooncake and offloading connectors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64096">#64096</a>); UMBP standalone mode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61795">#61795</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61793">#61793</a>).</li>
<li>Out-of-tree and LMCache MP connectors with the extensible KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66605">#66605</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66674">#66674</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>KV offload</strong>
<ul>
<li>SimpleCPUOffload with one host buffer per rank and opt-in HugeTLB pages (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60130">#60130</a>); no zero-filled pool (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64345">#64345</a>).</li>
<li>KVCR v0.2.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64224">#64224</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63889">#63889</a>); async lookups published per request group (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57474">#57474</a>); secondary tiers rejected on multi-node engines (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62930">#62930</a>).</li>
<li>Hardened P2P tier (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63781">#63781</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64250">#64250</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64256">#64256</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64251">#64251</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64501">#64501</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63618">#63618</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64300">#64300</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63988">#63988</a>) and correctness fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64229">#64229</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63566">#63566</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65279">#65279</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65631">#65631</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64400">#64400</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63937">#63937</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65640">#65640</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>HiSparse</strong>
<ul>
<li>Uses the extensible KV cache by default: 16% more GPU+host KV capacity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65214">#65214</a>).</li>
<li>Fused slot invalidation, up to 1.8% lower TPOT on GLM-5.3-NVFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60103">#60103</a>); fewer hash probes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61263">#61263</a>); speculative-group prefetch (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63801">#63801</a>).</li>
<li>Platform backend for hardware plugins (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62592">#62592</a>); scheduler deadlock fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62979">#62979</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>EPD and distributed</strong>
<ul>
<li>Metadata-only image and video inputs for GLM-5.3-Flash and DeepSeek-V4.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64071">#64071</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62802">#62802</a>).</li>
<li>Bitwise-identical 1-stage and 2-stage custom all-reduce (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57042">#57042</a>); FlashInfer all-reduce gated on 16-byte rows (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63123">#63123</a>).</li>
<li>Cross-node MNNVL groups kept off CUDA IPC all-reduce (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/55835">#55835</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<b>Hardware</b>
<ul>
<li><strong>NVIDIA</strong>
<ul>
<li>Step5 NVFP4 TRTLLM-Gen MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65152">#65152</a>).</li>
<li>DeepSeek-V4.1: latency_specialized decode extended, 5.9–17% faster decode step on GB300 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63921">#63921</a>); swap-AB CuTe DSL decode attention for TP2/TP4, 1.4–2.15× (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62633">#62633</a>); opt-in fused DSpark Markov sampling, 1.6–3.9× (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64442">#64442</a>).</li>
<li>Sparse-indexer top-k reads BF16 logits directly (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62172">#62172</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64138">#64138</a>); single-launch fused prefill top-k (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60618">#60618</a>).</li>
<li>GLM-5.3 TMA MoE projections on H200 and SM100 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63820">#63820</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65746">#65746</a>); SM90 native small-head sparse FlashMLA, +10% on GLM-5.3-Flash TP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64510">#64510</a>).</li>
<li>CuTe DSL BF16x3 router GEMM for fp32 routers on Hopper (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61903">#61903</a>); fused GDN MTP decode for DSpark K=8, +18% on B300 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65874">#65874</a>).</li>
<li>GLM-5.3-Flash on 2× DGX Spark, 15% lower TPOT (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62956">#62956</a>); faster weight loading on integrated GPUs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58868">#58868</a>).</li>
<li>Rubin locality-domain NVFP4 MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61685">#61685</a>); Jetson Thor (SM110) fixes (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62133">#62133</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65050">#65050</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62275">#62275</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>AMD ROCm</strong>
<ul>
<li>ROCm 10.0 only (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939">#61939</a>); AITER v0.1.28.post1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63774">#63774</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65937">#65937</a>); MoRI EPv2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61361">#61361</a>).</li>
<li>MonoKernel decode for Muse Spark Open DSpark verify (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63440">#63440</a>), DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash at TP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64101">#64101</a>) and the MiniMax-M3.1 DSpark drafter, +10% P90 interactivity (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65386">#65386</a>).</li>
<li>Kimi-K3 specialized decode (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61435">#61435</a>), AITER-tuned prefill GEMMs: mean TTFT 5.35 → 4.05 s (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63730">#63730</a>), opt-in FlyDSL FP8 MLA prefill (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59124">#59124</a>).</li>
<li>DeepSeek-V4-Pro P/D over MoRIIO (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/48989">#48989</a>); Step-5-Preview (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61837">#61837</a>) and Reflection Beam 1.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62057">#62057</a>) on ROCm.</li>
<li>RDNA: TRITON_ATTN by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60418">#60418</a>), AITER Triton kernels on RDNA3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52970">#52970</a>), HIP GDN prefill backend for gfx1151 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56307">#56307</a>), opt-in W4A16 dense prefill cache: TTFT −22 to −26% on Strix Halo (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60710">#60710</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: Kimi-K2.6 int4 prefill regression on Triton 3.8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62565">#62565</a>), MI355 extensible KV cache OOMs (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65418">#65418</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Intel XPU</strong>
<ul>
<li>PyTorch 2.15 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63977">#63977</a>) and vllm_xpu_kernels 0.1.17 with new MoE/MLA interfaces (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60609">#60609</a>).</li>
<li>Per-token dynamic FP8 activations (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61362">#61362</a>); faster batched Triton MoE (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51605">#51605</a>); MRoPE kernel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59938">#59938</a>); GDN metadata reuse (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64479">#64479</a>); DeepSeek-V4 adaptive verification (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62309">#62309</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>CPU</strong>
<ul>
<li>MRV2 on CPU backends without Triton: Arm, IBM Z, PowerPC, RISC-V (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61995">#61995</a>).</li>
<li>zentorch 2.14.0.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65669">#65669</a>) and Laguna MoE on Zen (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63711">#63711</a>).</li>
<li>Faster GDN spec-decode updates (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64829">#64829</a>); Gemma 4 MoE gelu_tanh (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61593">#61593</a>); bidirectional encoder-only attention (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63738">#63738</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Other:</strong> out-of-tree layer overrides via <code>vllm.model_executor.hw_agnostic.resolve</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60218">#60218</a>); shared routed-expert row restorers (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64689">#64689</a>); Ascend Triton fix (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64013">#64013</a>).</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<b>Quantization</b>
<ul>
<li>New <code>--quantization lut_b_moe</code>: about 3.14-bit MoE experts with MXFP8 activations, native on Rubin (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62759">#62759</a>).</li>
<li><code>w4afp8</code> checkpoints on the W4A8 FP8 MoE path (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64410">#64410</a>).</li>
<li>Block Hadamard input rotation with online <code>mxfp4_hadamard32</code> and Quark online-rotation checkpoints (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62750">#62750</a>).</li>
<li>CSF-compressed NVFP4 block scales in ModelOpt (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64312">#64312</a>); activation quant overrides for online MXFP4 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/51808">#51808</a>).</li>
<li>DeepSeek-V4.1 MXFP8 linears on cuBLASLt (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64815">#64815</a>); 3× faster MXFP4 activation quant (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63101">#63101</a>); faster FP8 quant on SM100+ (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61641">#61641</a>).</li>
<li>QuIP NVFP4 on Humming (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65230">#65230</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: NVFP4 NaN logits (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63382">#63382</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61755">#61755</a>), Humming FP8 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60163">#60163</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56606">#56606</a>), hadacore (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63514">#63514</a>).</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<b>API and frontend</b>
<ul>
<li><strong>New endpoints and options</strong>
<ul>
<li><code>/checkpoint/prepare</code>, <code>/checkpoint/status</code>, <code>/checkpoint/restore</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62570">#62570</a>).</li>
<li><code>thinking_token_budget</code> on <code>/v1/responses</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63896">#63896</a>); <code>/v1/completions</code> in <code>vllm run-batch</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64097">#64097</a>).</li>
<li>Streaming derender on <code>/inference/v1/generate</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63103">#63103</a>).</li>
<li>Anthropic Messages API: <code>min_tokens</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65459">#65459</a>) and <code>thinking.type=between_tools</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65954">#65954</a>).</li>
<li><code>vllm:request_render_duration_seconds</code> histogram (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60213">#60213</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Tool calling and reasoning</strong>
<ul>
<li>New <code>granite_toolcall_parser</code> for Granite 4.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65216">#65216</a>); llama3_json moved to the ParserEngine (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63569">#63569</a>).</li>
<li>Incremental streaming for Kimi K3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62203">#62203</a>), <code>tool_choice="required"</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60882">#60882</a>), DeepSeek V4/V3.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/58780">#58780</a>), GLM-4.7 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64535">#64535</a>), Qwen3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64675">#64675</a>).</li>
<li>Fixes: DeepSeek V4.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62517">#62517</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64971">#64971</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62729">#62729</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62647">#62647</a>), GLM-4.7 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64615">#64615</a>), Step3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65274">#65274</a>), FunctionGemma (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63992">#63992</a>), union/enum string parameters (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64518">#64518</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Behavior</strong>
<ul>
<li>Corrupt or oversized images and disabled local media return 400 instead of 500 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63433">#63433</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60308">#60308</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64032">#64032</a>).</li>
<li>Responses accepts incomplete assistant/reasoning history, e.g. from Codex (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/35508">#35508</a>).</li>
<li>Multi-template processors use the default template (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63606">#63606</a>); an empty <code>tools</code> array is accepted like OpenAI (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65547">#65547</a>).</li>
<li>The first streamed usage reports cached prompt tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65036">#65036</a>); plain completion stream frames render about 9× faster (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52875">#52875</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Rust frontend</strong>
<ul>
<li>Stateless Responses API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63902">#63902</a>) with namespace tools (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65853">#65853</a>); <code>n &gt; 1</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65625">#65625</a>); <code>--skip-tokenizer-init</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64697">#64697</a>).</li>
<li>Hybrid and external DP load balancing (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64451">#64451</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64450">#64450</a>); native GLM-5.3 chat rendering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62424">#62424</a>).</li>
<li>Prompt block hashing off the engine's GIL (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63842">#63842</a>); <code>--reasoning-effort-map</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62312">#62312</a>).</li>
<li><code>--enable-log-outputs</code>, <code>--max-log-len</code>, <code>--root-path</code>, <code>--log-error-stack</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63769">#63769</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64223">#64223</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64222">#64222</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65001">#65001</a>).</li>
<li>More request, response and metric parity with Python (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65293">#65293</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65294">#65294</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65623">#65623</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65624">#65624</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64218">#64218</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64526">#64526</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63964">#63964</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65636">#65636</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<b>Security, dependencies and build</b>
<ul>
<li><strong>Security</strong>
<ul>
<li>ReDoS in the validation-error sanitizer fixed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/52120">#52120</a>).</li>
<li>FunAudioChat audio bounded to the profiled duration (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57411">#57411</a>).</li>
<li><code>/v1/systemone</code> honors <code>--trust-request-chat-template</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64503">#64503</a>).</li>
<li>Raised floors for filelock, OpenTelemetry, graphql-core and others (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63574">#63574</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Dependencies</strong>
<ul>
<li>PyTorch stays at 2.14.0. FlashInfer 0.7.3 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65930">#65930</a>).</li>
<li><code>huggingface_hub &gt;= 2.0.0</code>, <code>tokenizers &gt;= 0.23.3</code>, <code>timm &gt;= 1.0.30</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64779">#64779</a>); <code>transformers &gt;= 5.16.1, &lt; 5.23.0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64049">#64049</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64529">#64529</a>); <code>mistral-common &gt;= 1.13.0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64169">#64169</a>).</li>
<li>DeepEP 2.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/59844">#59844</a>); DeepGEMM pin updates (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64411">#64411</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64580">#64580</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65494">#65494</a>); FlashMLA and FlashKDA pins (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61971">#61971</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64759">#64759</a>).</li>
<li>NIXL 1.5.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64579">#64579</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64585">#64585</a>); mooncake-transfer-engine 0.3.14 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65576">#65576</a>); Run:ai Model Streamer 0.17.1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63174">#63174</a>); <code>fastokens &gt;= 0.3.5</code> for <code>VLLM_USE_FASTOKENS</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65679">#65679</a>).</li>
<li>ROCm: AITER v0.1.27 → v0.1.28.post1 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63774">#63774</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65937">#65937</a>). XPU: PyTorch 2.15 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63977">#63977</a>), triton-xpu 3.9.0 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65047">#65047</a>), vllm_xpu_kernels 0.1.17 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60609">#60609</a>). CPU: zentorch 2.14.0.2 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65669">#65669</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Build</strong>
<ul>
<li>x86_64 CUDA wheel about 82 MB (about 25%) smaller, kernels unchanged (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65518">#65518</a>).</li>
<li>Default CUDA image installs the AWS EFA userspace; the <code>-efa</code> image now only adds DeepEP for EFA (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65576">#65576</a>); nightly EFA images as <code>vllm/vllm-openai:efa-nightly</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64028">#64028</a>).</li>
<li>One ROCm build and release pipeline, ROCm 10.0 only (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939">#61939</a>).</li>
<li>Source builds fetch <code>triton_kernels</code> from a tarball (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65162">#65162</a>) and ship tuned kernel configs in the wheel (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64063">#64063</a>).</li>
<li>CUDA <code>x.yf</code> arches no longer fall back to a lower minor (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66016">#66016</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</details>
<details>
<b>All breaking changes, deprecations and new defaults</b>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V1 removed</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373">#63373</a>).
<ul>
<li><code>VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER</code> and <code>VllmConfig.use_v2_model_runner</code> are gone; setting the env var to anything but <code>1</code> fails at startup.</li>
<li>Dropped with it: the V1 logits-processor API (<code>vllm.v1.worker.gpu.sample.logits_processor</code> is the only interface), the <code>custom_class</code> speculative method, <code>use_heterogeneous_vocab</code>, <code>disable_padded_drafter_batch</code>, <code>parallel_drafting</code> outside DFlash/DSpark, PP with <code>external_launcher</code>, <code>NGramPerReqLogitsProcessor</code>, and x86 CPUs without Triton.</li>
<li>Deprecated: <code>parallel_drafting</code>, <code>capture_torch_profiler</code>, <code>enable_layerwise_nvtx_tracing</code>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Default <code>--block-size</code> is 64</strong> instead of 16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231">#63231</a>). Backends with a preferred block size still override it (ROCm paged attention keeps 16), and an explicit <code>--block-size</code> still wins.</li>
<li><strong><code>gpu_memory_utilization</code> defaults to 1.0</strong> when unset with the extensible KV cache on (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a>). An explicit value in (0.8, 1.0) that withholds 1 GiB or more logs a warning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66704">#66704</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ROCm 7.2</strong> wheels and <code>-rocm72</code> images are no longer published (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939">#61939</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Removed:</strong>
<ul>
<li>QuTLASS and the <code>fp_quant</code> method (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65230">#65230</a>).</li>
<li>Marlin INT8/FP8-activation (W4A8) paths and <code>VLLM_MARLIN_INPUT_DTYPE</code>; use <code>--quantization humming</code> with <code>VLLM_HUMMING_INPUT_QUANT_CONFIG</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65542">#65542</a>).</li>
<li>Keye-VL and Keye-VL-1.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64287">#64287</a>); <code>disable_mtp_completeness_check</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49459">#49459</a>); the <code>refresh_derived_weights</code> hook (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65914">#65914</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Dependencies:</strong> <code>huggingface_hub &gt;= 2.0.0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64779">#64779</a>); <code>mistral-common &gt;= 1.13.0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64169">#64169</a>); XPU requires vllm_xpu_kernels 0.1.17 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60609">#60609</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model and plugin code:</strong> all <code>process_weights_after_loading</code> hooks share one signature and the loader runs every module's hook (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57678">#57678</a>); config <code>compute_hash()</code> replaced by <code>compile_factors()</code> / <code>hash_config()</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65652">#65652</a>).</li>
<li><strong>API behavior:</strong>
<ul>
<li>llama3_json no longer streams tool arguments incrementally (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63569">#63569</a>).</li>
<li>Canary transcription without <code>language</code> returns 400 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65445">#65445</a>).</li>
<li>An empty <code>tools</code> array is accepted in both frontends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65547">#65547</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65854">#65854</a>).</li>
<li><code>/v1/systemone</code> rejects request-supplied chat templates without <code>--trust-request-chat-template</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64503">#64503</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA adapters on modules vLLM does not wrap are refused (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63489">#63489</a>); misconfigured NCCL weight transfer raises (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57022">#57022</a>).</li>
<li><code>vllm-rs serve</code> no longer reads <code>--data-parallel-rank</code> from <code>VLLM_DP_RANK</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65369">#65369</a>).</li>
<li>AITER fused shared experts with MiniMax M3 and DeepSeek V4 need <code>VLLM_ROCM_USE_AITER=1</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61457">#61457</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Still deprecated:</strong> <code>--mamba-cache-mode</code> has no effect; its removal was postponed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64838">#64838</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New defaults:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Extensible KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a>); block size 64 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231">#63231</a>); best-effort NUMA binding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61258">#61258</a>).</li>
<li>Token-sharded sparse-MLA prefill at 1024 or more prefill tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63956">#63956</a>); MLA decode up-projection before the A2A merge (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64994">#64994</a>).</li>
<li><code>VLLM_COMPILE_ON_ONE_RANK</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63247">#63247</a>); minimum valid EPLB <code>num_redundant_experts</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30614">#30614</a>).</li>
<li>HiSparse on the extensible KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65214">#65214</a>); encoder CUDA graphs up to <code>encoder_cudagraph_max_token_budget=5120</code>, eager above (#65227).</li>
<li>TRITON_ATTN on RDNA3/RDNA4 (#60418); MRV2 on non-Triton CPUs (#61995); <code>float32</code> Mamba SSM cache for Nemotron Omni (#63218); Zipf routing in <code>vllm bench forward</code> (#64167).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>

</details>
